Petition under Article 226 of the Constitution of lr dia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, tl-e High Court may be
pleased to issue a Writ of Mandamus or any other app'opriate Writ, Order or
direction
a) declaring the action of the Respondents in not calling the Candidates, whose
names were included in the Provisional Selection List in 1:2 Ration, for
Certificate Verification, in the descending order of the Po:;ts viz., Degree College
Lecturers, Junior College Lecturers, Post Graduate Teachers, Trained Graduate
Teachers (for short DL, JL, PGT and TGT Posts) etr;. in various subjects,
pursuant to the Notifications No. 1 of 2023, 2 of 2023, 3 of 2023 and 9 of 2023,
all are dated 0510412023, issued by the 4th Respondent, by seeking their option
for relinquishment of Posts, in the case when the CanciJates were selected for
2
J
4
5
6
7
8

?t
Higher Posts as well as Lower Posts simultaneously, instead of keeping the
Lower Cadre Vacancies / Posts as Backlog Vacancies / Posts, contrary to the
dicta of the Honourable Supreme.Court in the case of Munja Praveen and Others
Vs. State of Telangana and Others, reported in 2017 (14) SCC 797,
b) declaring the action of the Respondents No. 3 and 4 in not following the
transparent procedure for selection of the Candidates, by displaying the Zone
wise Cut off marks, reservation wise Cut off marks in the 'l:2 Ratio in the
Provisional List for Verification of Certificates and in the l:1 Ratio Provisional
Selection List, as arbitrary, illegal and violative of Articles 14, 16 and 21 of Lhe
Constitutlon of lndia and
c) Consequently direct the Respondents, to obtain the relinquishment of the
Candidates, by seeking their option for relinquishment of Posts, in the case when
the Candidates were selected for Higher Posts as well as Lower Posts
simultaneously, viz., Degree College Lecturers, Junior College Lecturers, Post
Graduate Teachers, Trained Graduate Teachers (for short DL, JL, PGT and TGT
Postg) etc. in various subjects, instead of keeping the Lower Cadre Vacancies /
Posts as Backlog Vacancies / Posts, pursuant to the Notifications No. 1of 2023,
2 of 2023,3 of 2023 and I of 2023, all are dated 0510412023, issued by the 4th
Respondent, by following the dicta of the Honourable Supreme Court in the case
of [\/unja Praveen and Others Vs. State of Telangana and Others, reported in
2017 (14\ SCC 797, with all consequential benefits.

.-' +{
a) declaring the action of the Respondents in not calling the candidates, whose
names were included in the Provisional selection List in '1 :2 Ratio, for certificate
Verification, in the descending order of the Posts viz., Deg ree College Lecturers,
Junior College Lecturers, Post Graduate Teachers, (for :;hort DL, JL and PGT
posts) etc. in various subjects, pursuant to the Notifications No. 1 of 2023,2 of
2023 and 3 of 2023, all are dated O5-O4-2O23 ' issued by the 4th Respondent, by
seeking their option for relinquishment of Posts, in the cas,: when the candidates
were selected for Higher Posts as well as Lower Posts sirr ultaneously, instead of
keeping the Lower Cadre vacancies / Posts as Backlog Vacancies / Posts,
contrary to the dicta of the Hon'ble Supreme Court in the ,:,ase of Ir/unja Praveen
and Others Vs. State of Telangana and Others, reported in 2017 (14) SCC 797'
and not considering the Petitioner's case for appointmont as Junior Lecturer
(Botany), pursuant to the Notification No. 2 of 2023 ' a:; arbitrary, illegal and
violative of the Articles '14, 16 and 21 of the Constitution o. lndia,
b) declaring the action of the Respondents No.3 and 4 in not following the
transparent procedure for selection of the candidates, t)y displaying the zone
wise Cut off marks, reservation wise Cut off marks in l1e '1 :2 Ratio in in the
Provisional List for Verification of certificates and in th<: 1:1 Ratio Provisional
Selection List, as arbitrary, illegal and violative of Articler; 14, 16 and 21 of the
Constitution of lndia and
c) Consequently direct the Respondents, to obtain the relinquishment of the
Candidates, by seeking their option for relinquishment of I)osts, in the case when
the Candidates were selected for Higher Posts as well as Lower Posts
simultaneously, viz., Degree College Lecturers, Junior (l'ollege Lecturers, Post

downwards and by following the dicta of the Hon'ble Supreme Court in the case
of lVunja Praveen and Others Vs. State of Telangana ernd Others, reported in

Petition under Article 226 of the Constitution of lniia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, thr:, High Court may be
pleased to issue a Writ of Mandamus or any other apprcpriate Writ, Order or
direction
a). declaring the action of the Respondents in not calling 1le Candidates, whose
names were included in the Provisional Selection List in 1:2 Ratio, for Certificate

+1
Verif ication, in the descending order of the Posts viz., Degree college Lecturers,
Junior College Lecturers, Post Graduate Teachers and Trained Graduate
Teachers (for short DL, JL, PGT and TGT Posts) etc. in various subjects,
pursuant to the Notifications No. 1 of 2023,2 of 2023,3 of 2O23 and 9 of 2023,
all are dated 05-04-2023, issued by the 4th Respondent, by seeking their option
for relinquishment of Posts, in the case when the candidates were selected for
Higher Posts as well as Lower Posts simultaneously, instead of keeping the
Lower cadre vacancies / Posts as Backlog vacancies / Posts, contrary to the
dicta of the Hon'ble supreme court in the case of Munja Praveen and others vs.
State of Telangana and Others, reported in 2017 (14) SCC 797, and not
considering the Petitioner's case for appointment as PGT (Telugu) / TGT
(Telugu), pursuant to the Notification No. 2 of 2023 and 9 of 2023, dated 05-04-
2023, as arbitrary, illegal and violative of the Articles 14, 16 and 21 of the
Constitution of lndia,
b). declaring the action of the Respondents No. 3 and 4 in not following the
transparent procedure for selection of the candidates, by displaying the Zone
wise cut off marks, reservation wise cut off marks in the 1:2 Ratio in the
Provisional List for Verif ication of Certificates and in the 1 :1 Ratio Provisional
selection List, as arbitrary, illegal and violative of Articles 1 4, 16 and 2'1 of the
Constitution of lndia and
c). consequently direct the Respondents, to obtain the relinquishment of the
candidates, by seeking their option for relinquishment of Posts, in the case when
the candidates were selected for Higher Posts as well as Lower Posts
simultaneously, viz., Degree college Lecturers, Junior college Lecturers, Post
Graduate Teachers and Trained Graduate Teachers (for short DL, JL, PGT and
TGT Posts) etc. in various subjects, pursuant to the Notifications No. 1 oI 2023,2
oI 2023, 3 ot 2023 and 9 of 2023, all are dated 05-04-2023' issued by the 4th
Respondent, by operating the Merit List downwards and by following the dicta of
the Hon'ble Supreme Court in the case of Munja Praveen and others Vs. State
of Telangana and others, reported in 2o17 (14) SCC 797, and accordingly
consider the Petitioners' cases for appointment as PGT (Telugu) / TGT (Telugu),
pursuant to the (i) Notification No. 2 of 2023 for the notified Junior Lecturer /

Physical Director / Librarian in the Junior colleges and (i) Notification No. 3 of
2023 for the notified Post Graduate Teachers, (iii) Notificzrtion No. 9 of 2023 for
the notified Trained Graduate Teachers, all are dated 05,04-2023, issued by the
4th Respondent, against the vacancies that are caused cue to relinquishment /
deemed relinquishment of the Lower Posts by the candicates who joined in the
Higher Posts, with all consequential benefits.

Petition under Article 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
crrcumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue writ, direction or order more particularly one in nature of writ of
mandamus under Article 226 of Constitution of lndia declaring the action of the
Respondents in not operating the Merit list downwards and in not recruiting the
Petitioners in the Fall out vacancies which are created due to relinquishment and
non-joining of the candidates pursuant to the Notification No.1/2023 , 0212023,
0312023,0412023,0512023 and Ogl2023 all dated 05-04-2023 as illegal, arbitrary
and violative of article 14 and 21 of lndian Constitution and consequently direct
the Respondents to consider the Petitioners candidature in filling up the fall out
vacancies as per their merit in the present notification No.1/2023, O212O23,
0312023,0412023,0512023 and Ogl2O23 all dated 0510412023 in accordance with
law and in the interests of justice.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated
in the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High Court may be pleased to
direct the Respondents to stay further recruitment proceedings pursuant to the
notification No.1/2023, 0212023, 0312023, 0412023, 0512023 and Ogl2023 all
dated 0510412023 pending disposal of the main writ petition in the interests of
justice.

Petition under Article 226 of lhe Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue a writ, order, or direction, more particularly one in the nature of
Writ of [/andamus or any other appropriate writ, order or direction.
a) To declare the action of the Respondents in not calling the Candidates whose
names were included in the Provisional Selection List in 1.2 Ratio for Certificate
Verification, in the descending order of the Posts viz., Degree College Lecturers,
Junior College Lecturers, Post Graduate Teachers, and Trained Graduate
Teachers (for short DL, JL, PGT and TGT Posts) etc. in various subjects,
pursuant to the Notifications No. 1 of 2023,2 of 2023,3 of 2023 and 9 of 2023,
all dated 0510412023, issued by the 4th Respondent, by seeking their option for
relinquishment of Posts, in case when the Candidates were selected for Higher
Posts as well as Lower Posts simultaneously, instead of keeping the Lower
Cadre Vacancies/Posts as Backlog Vacancies/Posts, as arbitrary, illegal and
violative of the directives of the Honorable Supreme Court in the case of Munja
Praveen and Others Vs. State of Telangana and Others, reported in 2017 (14\
SCC 797.
b) To declare the action of Respondents No.3 and 4 in not following the
transparent procedure for selection of the Candidates, by displaying the Zone-wise Cut-off marks and reservation-wise Cut-off marks in lhe 1.2 Ratio in the
Provisional List for Verification of Certificates and in the 1.1 Ratio Provisional
Selection List, as arbitrary, illegal and violative of Articles 14, 16 and 21 of the
Constitution of lndia.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated
in the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High Court may be pleased to
direct the Respondents to obtain the relinquishment of the Candidates, by
seeking their option for relinquishment of Posts, in cases where the Candidates
were selected for Higher Posts as well as Lower Posts simultaneously, viz.'
Degree College Lecturers, Junior College Lecturers, Post Graduate Teachers,
Trained Graduate Teachers (for short DL, JL, PGT and TGT Posts) etc. in
various subjects, instead of keeping the Lower Cadre Vacancies/Posts as
Backlog Vacancies/Posts, pursuant to the Notifications No. 1 ol 2023,2 of 2023
'
3 of 2023 and 9 of 2023, all daled 0510412023, issued by the 4th Respondent, by
following the dicta of the Honourable Supreme Court in the case of Munja
Praveen and Others Vs. State of Telangana and Others, reported in 2017 (14)
SCC 797, with all consequential benefits pending disposal of the above writ
petition.

Petition under Article 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue a Writ of Mandamus
a) To declare the action of the Respondents in not calling t're Candidates whose
names were included in the Provisional Selection List in 1:il Ratio for Certrficate
Verification, in the descending order of the Posts viz., Degrr>e College Lecturers,
Junior College Lecturers, Post Graduate Teachers, an,l Trained Graduate
Teachers (for short DL, JL, PGT and TGT Posts) etc. in various subjects,
pursuant to the Notifications No. 1 of 2023, 2 o'f 2023,3 ct 2023 and 9 of 2023,
all dated 0510412023, issued by the 4th Respondent, by se,eking their option for
relinquishment of Posts, in case when the Candidates we rr: selected for Higher
Posts as well as Lower Posts simultaneously, instead c1' keeping the Lower
Cadre Vacancies/Posts as Backlog Vacancies/Posts, as arbitrary, illegal and
violative of the directives of the Hon'ble Supreme Courl r the case of lvlunja
Praveen and Others Vs. State of Telangana and Others. .eported in 2017 (14)
SCC 797.
b) To declare the action of Respondents No.3 and l.in not following the
transparent procedure for selection of the Candidates, b1r d isplaying the Zone-wise Cut-off marks and reservation-wise Cut-off marks in the 1.2 Ratio in the
Provisional List for Verification of Certificates and in the 'l .1 Ratio Provisional
Selection List, as arbitrary, illegal and violative of Articles 14, 16 and 21 of the
Constitution of lndia.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated
in the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High CoL ( may be pleased to
direct the Respondents to obtain the relinquishment of the Candidates, by
seeking their option for relinquishment of Posts, in cases v;here the Candidates
were selected for Higher Posts as well as Lower Posts simultaneously, viz.,
Degree College Lecturers, Junior College Lecturers, Post Graduate Teachers,
Trained Graduate Teachers (for short DL, JL, PGT an,l TGT Posts) etc. in
various subjects, instead of keeping the Lower Cadre Vacancies/Posts as
1@
,l, t

tq 28
Backlog Vacancies/Posts, pursuant to the Notifications No. 1 of 2023,2 of 2023,
3 of 2023 and 9 of 2023, all dated 05/04/2023, issued by the 4th Respondent, by
following the dicta of the Honourable Supreme Court in the case of Munja
Praveen and Others Vs. State of Telangana and Others, reported in 2017 (14)
SCC 797, with all consequential benefits pending disposal of the above writ
petition.

Petition under Article 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue writ, direction or order more particularly one in nature of writ of
mandamus declaring the action of the Respondents in not operating the tvlerit
List downwards and in not recruiting the Petitioner in the fall out vacancies which
are created due to relinquishment and non-joining of the candidates pursuant to
the Notification No. 1i2023, 212023,312023,412023, 512023 and 912023 all dated
05-04-2023 as illegal, arbitrary.and violative of Article 14 and.21 of lndian
Constitution, and consequently direct the Respondents to consider the
Petitioners candidature in filling up the fall out vacancies as per their merit in the
present Notification No. 1/2023, 212023, 312023, 412023, 512023 and 912O23 all
dated 05-04-2023 in accordance with law and in the interest of justice.

laid by the Hon'ble Apex Court in Civil Appeal (CA) No. 10583-'10565 of 2017,
besides being violation of Article 14, 16 and 21 of the Constitution of lndia and
consequently direct the respondents to consider the cases of the petitioners in

which obtained by them in the process of selection in pursuant to the Notiftcation
Nos. '1 to 4 and 9 o'f 2023, dated 05-04-2023.

COMMON ORDER:
The issue involved in these Writ Petitions is,
respondent authorities not operating the merit list
downwards and not recruiting the petitioners in the fallout
vacancles.
2. All these Writ Petitions hereby disposed of by this
Common Order, as the issue involved in these Writ
Petitions is identical.

Sri Siva, learned Senior counsel representing Smt'

for the petitioners.

Bhoopal Reddy, learned Standing CotL.rsel for State
Residential Educational Society, counsel a1>pearing for the
respondents
4. For the sake of convenience, thr: facts in Wp
No.31745 of 2024 are discussed hereunder
Learned Senior Counsel Sri Siva, reltresenting Goda
Rama Lakshmi, learned counsel for the per. tioner, submits
that the 3.d respondent authorities on 05.O4.2023, calling
for applications from eligible candidates for being recruited
to various posts viz. Lecturer/physical Dircctor/Librarial
in Degree Colleges (Notification No.1 120231, Junior
Lecturers/Physicat Directors/Librarians in,Junior Colleges
in Residential Educational Institutions Societies
(Notiflrcation No.2/2023), post Gradual.r: Teachers in
Residential Educational Institutions Societies (Notif,rcation
No.3 /20231, Librarian (School) in Residentia_l Educational
Institutions Societies (Notification No.4/L!O231, physical
Director (School)/Physical Director Grade,It in Residential
2

Educational Institutions Societies (Notification No.S/ 2023)
and Trained Graduate Teachers in Residentia,l Educational
Institutions Societies (Notihcation No.9 / 2023) .
5. Learned Senior counsel further submits that the
petitioners herein were eligible for substantial number of
posts proposed to be filled up by the above Notihcations.
They have responded to the sarne. The selection process
comprised of a compr.rter based test which was held
between 1"t and 23'd August, 2023. It is pertinent to
rnention here that so far as Degree Lecturers, Junior
Lecturers and Post Graduate Teachers are concerned,
Paper-I was conducted simultaneously. The said paper
being general studies, it may be only for the purpose of
convenience instead of setting more than one paper for
testing the ability of the candidates.
6. Learned Senior counsel further submits that in
respect of Junior Lecturers and Post Graduate Teachers,
paper-Il which was pedago$/ was conducted commonly'
The 3.d paper was independently conducted for all the
posts as it related to their respective subjects for which the

4
recruitment is to be held. It is also pertinerLt to state there
was no mrnimum marks prescribed for ea<:h of the paper
and selections were to be made based or: the aggregate
marks securcd by the candidates
7. Learned Senior counsel further sutlrnits that it is
common prudency that could be reasonabl., expected from
a Board given the responsibility of condr-t,-:ting selections
and recommending candidates for variotrs posts in the
hierarchy starting from a Trained Graduar_e Teacher (the
lower position for which Notification has lteen issued bv
the 3.d respondent Board) and the highesr. position being
that of Degree Lecturers to declare resr-r. ts from top to
bottom for effectively filling up of alt the vacalcies.
8. Learned Senior counsel further subrnits that it the
results f<rr the lowest post of T(;'t' is declared
simultaneously with that of a Degree [,ecturers, there
could be a possibility of a candidate behind selected
against both the posts. In such a situa_l ion, one of the
posts could not be filled up and that obvi:rusly, would be
the post lower in the hierarchy. In those <:,rcumstances, it

5
would be necessarily call for reviewing the entire situation
based on the number of people who do not report to
certilrcate verihcation and/or further selection process like
demonstration etc.,
g. Learned Senior counsel further submits that in the
instant case, the schedule for certificate verihcation (list
containing candidates in the ratio of l:2 i'e' two
candidates for every vacancy to be filled up)' In respect of
Post Graduate Teacher was fixed on 9th February,
Librarian (school), Physicat Director (school) on 8th
Februar5l, Trained Graduate Teacher on 25th, Junior
Lecturer on 16th and Degree Lecturer on 15th February,
2023. The final list (1:1) in so far as post graduate
teacher; Librarian (schoot), Physical Director (school),
librarians (Degree college), Physical Director (Degree
College), Librarian (Junior College), and Physical Director
(Junior College) was hnalized and appointment orders to

6
10. A considerable number of people havt: been selected
to more than one post and obviously as it is impossible to
report to al1 the posts to which they havr: been selected,
only one vacarlcy would be filled up and ot h er vacancies to
which the candidates have been selectr:rl would go a
begging. This can never be the purpost, of issuing of
Notihcations. Unfortunately, this very crur:ial aspect has
been lost sight of the 3.d respondent Boa rd resulting in
forcing the unemployed youth to knock t h e doors of this
Hon'ble Court for justice.
11. Learned Senior counsel further sul.rmits that the
recruiting authorities have been relying r rpon the State
Government instructions as constrained in G.O.Ms. No.g1
G.A.(Ser.A), Department, dated 22.O,2.t997 and
G.O.Ms.No.544, G.A.(Ser.A), Depar-trrrent, dated
04.12.1998. The purport of the G.Os would rnake it
necessary that the recruiting agency will h:rve to furnish a
list equal to the number of vacancies notified in the
Notification. In other words, in a recruittnent year, after
the selection of candidates and after issut: of appointment
order, if the candidates failed to join cLuty within the

7
stipulated period, that vacancy shall be riotified in the next
recruitment year. By virtue of the said G'Os, the system of
preparing a waiting list for fallout vacancies has been done
away with. In the latter G.O. referred to above' which is
an adhoc rule made in exerciseiof the powers conferred by
the proviso to Article 309 of the Constitution of India by
the Governor, it was made clear that fallout vacancies' if
any, due to relinquishment and non-joining etc'' of
selected candidates, shall be notifred in the next
recruitment.
12. Learned Senior counsel further submits that while
explaining the above said method, this principle may well
be applicable to cases where the recruitment agencies are
required to hold selections for filling up vacancies to a
particular post alone. But, it does not answer logic to
extend the said principle in cases where number of posts
atdifferentlevelsinhierarchyaresoughttobelrlledup.
The present case is a classic case where the application of
said principle has resulted in more than 18O0 of the
vacancies remaining unfilled. The result of application of
this principle and where results are declared either

8
simultaneously or ir-r respect of posts in ther lower category
earlier in point of time than the post in thr: lrigher echelons
of the hierarchy.
13. Learned Senior counsel further sulrmits that the
petitioners in the present Writ Petition have all been

but could not make up to 1:1 that is evenlrral selection for
issuance of appointment order. This is it direct result of
application of principle which cannot be r.rade appticable
in cases where more number of posts arr: sought to be
filled up. Since this is the only reason, wtr1, the petitioners
have not been eventua-lly selected, they arc constrained to
invoke the extraordinary jurisdiction of this Honble Court.

of 1017, dated

ANYALYSIS OF THE CASE LAWS AND
THE COURT:
FINDINGS OF

2r. Learned counsel for the petitioners and respondent
No.3 have relying upon the following cases and case laws:
1. In the Writ Appeal No.511 of 2021. The appeal is
hled against the order of the Single Judge of this Court in
W.P.No. 14926 of 2O2O. The issue involved in the case is
that, writ petitioners have participated in the process of
selection pursuant to the Notification dated 31'05'2OLA,
issued. by the present appellants/respondents in the main
Writ Petition in respect of as many as 1370 vacancies' The
learned Single Judge has passed the following order:
"g. This Court, houing considered the iual submi'ssions made
bg learned counsel for the respectiue parties, is of the
considered uieu that since the issue raised in these Wit
Petitions is squarelg couered bg the judgments rendered bg this
Court in W.P.No.7117 of 2O2O dated 11'08'2020 and
W.P.No.4495 of 2O19 dated 11.O6.2019 and as confirmed uide
judgment doted 03.O7.2O19 in W.A'No 551 of 2019' th'ese utit
petitions can be disposed of directing the htblic Seruice
Commission and the State Gouerrtment to fill up all the unfilled
uacancies stictly bg follouing Rule 6(A) of tlne Telongona State
Public Seruice Commission Rules' It is made clear that the
uocancies, ttthich tuere relinquished by the selected candidates'
ofter giuing appointment orders, those uacancies haue to be
tagged on to the next recnlitment notiJication and tle uacancies'
uLhictt are still lging uacant euen after issuonce of oppointment
orders, tlase uocancies are to be filled up stictlg bg follouing
13

1,4
Rule 6(A) of the Telangana State Public Szt uice Commission
Rules and the same should be offered to tltt nert meritorious
candidates in the order of meit."
Against the above said order, the present Writ
Appeal is filed. While dismissing the \\r:.it Appeal, the
Hontrle Division Bench of this Court marl,: the follo,.r,ing
Order:
"In the considered opinion of this Court, f:,ep -Lg in
uiew tlrc aforesaid judgment, once a uc :an<.tt is
aduertised and the uacancA is unfilted, as :t e person
in uhose fauour appointment order has br:r rr issued
has not joined-, the same cannot carry foru-'arrl to the
next selection. The next person in the ut:titing list
should be appointed against the uacancy. L,ou.teuer, in
cases uhere appointment orders utere lssur,r1, persons
haue joined and left thereafier, then the, ,.tttrticular
uaconcies haue to be carried forutard tc, the next
selection and therefore, this Court does nc,,t Jincl ang
reasorl to interfere with the order pass,zl bq the
learned Single Judge.
Accordingly, tlrc turit appeol is dismissed."
